## Source evidence and reading limits

All 57 distinct cited URLs in the pending rows were attempted. Fifty-one yielded complete PDF bodies, three yielded HTML responses, and three failed. Exact body hashes below make the retrieval evidence recoverable independently of temporary cache paths. Wall’s archived URL yielded an access response; its cited numbered passages were checked using the directly retrieved Auckland copy of the same named book. Hirsch’s archive response and the AMS book landing page were not treated as readable book chapters. Failed source retrievals were also checked through the web fetch path: the Nebraska and UFSCar sources remained unavailable; ScienceDirect returned no readable full text. Their affected rows remain owner decisions.

This dispatch read bounded relevant source statements/passages and inspected complete short arguments when adjudicating the specific source criticisms, including the three-page Francis exotic-sphere notes and the Mrowka transversal proof. It did not read every source book or certify all excluded proofs. The Ranz/Novikov source-gap allegations and inaccessible book passages are retained as owner source-route decisions where a fresh complete argument review is not established. PDF retrieval, automatic locator search and an old author report alone are never represented as a completed proof review; a search miss was not evidence of absence.
